Revert CHECK_EQ to DCHECK_EQ for extension icon width in
ContextMenuMatcher

Revert the CHECK_EQ check for icon.Width() in SetExtensionIcon back to
DCHECK_EQ to prevent crashes when extension icons do not match
gfx::kFaviconSize.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/4ce005a908400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

f91bbc3cc76c8ba17c6a391d06adc3998b78b10f [ diff ] 
ash: Migrate TabClusterUIClient to BrowserController::TabObserver

TabClusterUIClient should depend on the browser only through the
ash::BrowserController interface rather than on tab strip internals. It
already gets loading state changes from BrowserController::TabObserver;
this CL moves the remaining tab strip events there too and drops
BrowserTabStripTracker and TabStripModelObserver, along with their deps
and DEPS rules.

This preserves behavior:

The TabObserver events OnTabInserted, OnTabRemoved and OnTabReplaced
correspond one-to-one to the TabStripModelChange types the client
handled (kMoved and kSelectionOnly were ignored). BrowserControllerImpl
forwards them from the same TabStripModelObserver callbacks, observes
the same browsers (all of GlobalBrowserCollection), and reports a new
browser's existing tabs on creation, as BrowserTabStripTracker did.
The tracker's Init() also reported browsers existing at construction,
but there are none: TabClusterUIClient is created in PreProfileInit.

The browser is now passed in with each event, replacing the
GetBrowserForTab() lookup and its CHECK.

The one difference: when a browser closes, BrowserControllerImpl emits
OnTabRemoved for any tab still in its tab strip, whereas the tracker
only stopped observing. Such tabs' items are now removed instead of
lingering in TabClusterUIController.

WebView: Use android_webview trace category in AwBrowserContext

See crrev.com/c/8277073/comments/049ad4e4_553a7efb

`AwBrowserContext` can be initialized for non-default profiles outside
of startup. Replace the `"startup"` trace category with
`"android_webview"` in `AwBrowserContext::AwBrowserContext` and
`AwBrowserContext::CreateUserPrefService` for consistency.

TAG=agy
CONV=fa48e38d-c4b0-4cb4-b976-067852ce487e

00f66eff3e7189db3ad1b8f247509eec64cb8dd9 [ diff ] 
Ash: Migrate policy certificate tests off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

PolicyProvidedCertsOnUserSessionInitTest resolves the active user's
prepared browser context through BrowserContextHelper after session
startup. PolicyProvidedCertsForSigninExtensionTest checks its existing
sign-in browser context with the lower-level type predicate. The
browser-test target now depends directly on BrowserContextHelper.

[Vertical Tabs] Shift NTP instead of relaying out during resizing.

During a live drag resize, web pages are already only shifted, but
native pages (e.g. NTP) were relaid out on every drag move because of
margin and UiConfig inset updates. This made the NTP cards flicker on
tablets.

This CL updates CompositorViewHolder to set translationX on the native
page view and applies margins only on commit.

Before: http://screencast/cast/NjAxMDY4MDUxNDcwNzQ1NnxjNDEyMDBmYi00Ng
After: http://screencast/cast/NTgzODc1NTIyMTk5NTUyMHw5NWI1ZDhhNy05OA

b8f7e1e072557bff94b57f1121fc07fb3ccff95b
parent
771bd5560090c698fb77b7f104c8c96e12881f7d [ diff ] 
ChromeAndroidTask: Clear stale restored bounds once actions settle

setBoundsInDp() caches the requested bounds as the future restored
bounds, but nothing cleared the cache. A later user resize was
ignored by getRestoredBoundsInDp(). Clear the cache once all pending
actions settle, so WindowStateManager's OS-backed value is used.

Bug: 508071973, 482430429

[ChroMenu GlowUp] Block style cleanup

* Adds a 6px horizontal margin to block style elements
* Removes the title case variant of "New Isolated Window", which was
  inconsistent with the casing of other block style items
* For "New Isolated tab" and "New Isolated window" in sentence case,
  removes capitalization from "isolated". Our app menu UXer
  (tmessenger@) believes capitalization here is inappropriate, as
  "isolated" isn't considered to be a branded entity in the same way
  "Incognito" is.

ash: Show tooltip for inactive tab items in shelf app menu

With WindowPreviewOnShelf, items for inactive tabs have no window
preview, since the window only shows its active tab. Show a tooltip
'Inactive tab in "<window title>"' instead, in a bubble anchored
beside the menu on the same side as the preview bubble.

The tooltip is returned as the error of GetAppMenuItemWindow(); an
empty error shows neither a preview nor a tooltip.

Fri Oct 09 05:04:39 2026
tree
d82ec5d7f20a9f74d8f5fbd5aa774f6e78124ce8
parent
67c2880c6da2e3d716543015846bdcbd5c748fda [ diff ] 
storage: Prune unnecessary mojom public_deps in storage/public/mojom

//components/services/storage/public/mojom:mojom currently lists
//third_party/blink/public/mojom:mojom_core, :mojom_modules, and the
full //services/network/public/mojom target in public_deps.

However, none of the .mojom files in storage/public/mojom import
anything from mojom_core or mojom_modules, and they only import from
network's :url_loader_base and :mojom_network_param.

Pruning these dependencies allows storage/public/mojom to run
immediately after :mojom_platform on the mojom parser critical path
(bypassing mojom_core and mojom_modules). Also update
//components/services/storage/privileged/mojom:mojom to depend on
:mojom_modules directly (for indexeddb.mojom).

ChromeAndroidTask: Remove unused CLOSE and HIDE PendingActions

PendingAction.CLOSE and PendingAction.HIDE are no longer queued:
- ChromeAndroidTaskImpl#close() calls finishAndRemoveTask() directly.
- AndroidBaseWindow::Hide() is NOTREACHED().

Remove both constants and their handling from PendingActionManager.

[contextual-tasks] Add UMA logging for updated entry points.

- Histogram ContextualTasks.AskGoogle.Invoked is recorded with the
  invocation source when the Ask Google flow is invoked. This covers
  both side panel and omnibox routing. The invocation source
  distinguishes between the four possible entry points (context menu,
  app menu, toolbar icon, and homework action chip.)

  the side panel is closed after being opened by the Ask Google flow.

No UMA logging is being added to distinguish side panel/omnibox
routing, or whether the submenu is being used, as these are defined by
feature params for each experiment arm.

Original change's description:
> [ContextualTasks] Report context upload state
>
> Search Web previously had no visibility into whether newly added tab or
> Lens region context was still uploading or ready before query
> submission, preventing users from submitting queries with only newly
> added Chrome context and no text. Reporting `OnContextStateChanged`
> (`NONE`, `UPLOADING`, or `READY`) from
> `ContextualTasksWebContentsUserData` over the Search communication
> channel allows the AIM input plate to block submission while context
> uploads are in flight and enable it once at least one context item is
> ready.
>
> In-flight tab uploads take precedence over ready tabs, delayed tab
> snapshots, and mounted Lens chips so the reported state remains
> uploading until all pending uploads complete, and resets to none once
> the query is submitted or all next-turn context is removed.
>

mojo: Prune temporary imported kinds and values in translate.py

During _Module() translation, module.kinds and module.values are
populated with PRIMITIVES and all imported kinds and values (via
_Import) for symbol resolution. Because these temporary entries were not
pruned after translation, they remained in the serialized .mojom-module
pickles.

Prune module.kinds and module.values at the end of _Module() so only
definitions from the current module (plus MapKinds queried by C++
generators) are retained in the serialized module.

Across a clean rebuild of all 891 transitive .mojom-module dependencies
of //content/common:mojo_bindings and
//third_party/blink/public/mojom:mojom_platform, this reduces:
- Total .mojom-module size (891 files): 121.6 MB -> 80.3 MB (-34.0%)
- agent_scheduling_group.mojom-module: 5.61 MB -> 3.13 MB (-44.2%)
- shared_worker_host.mojom-module: 1.30 MB -> 966 KB (-25.7%)

Measured median wall time improvements (after clean .mojom-module
rebuild):
- //third_party/blink/public/mojom:mojom_platform__parser_action (249
  files):
  - Default parallelism (multi-core): 1.41s -> 1.19s (-0.22s, -15.6%)
  - Single-core (taskset -c 0): 6.31s -> 5.97s (-0.34s, -5.4%)
- //content/common:mojo_bindings__parser_action (18 files):
  - Default parallelism (multi-core): 1.64s -> 1.39s (-0.25s, -15.4%)
  - Single-core (taskset -c 0): 2.26s -> 1.87s (-0.40s, -17.5%)
- //content/common:mojo_bindings_shared__generator (18 files,
  single-threaded):
  0.84s -> 0.80s (-0.03s, -3.7%)

Affected test(s):
[://chrome/android\:chrome_public_test_apk!junit:org.chromium.chrome.browser.media.ui:AutoplayMutedNotificationTest#testUnmutedPlaybackTakesAudioFocus](https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/:%2F%2Fchrome%2Fandroid%5C:chrome_public_test_apk%21junit:org.chromium.chrome.browser.media.ui:AutoplayMutedNotificationTest%23testUnmutedPlaybackTakesAudioFocus?q=VHash%3A9d2db2838dc1d70b)

If this is a false positive, please report it at http://b.corp.google.com/createIssue?component=1199205&description=Analysis%3A+https%3A%2F%2Fci.chromium.org%2Fui%2Fp%2Fchromium%2Fbisection%2Ftest-analysis%2Fb%2F6534746768146432&format=PLAIN&priority=P3&title=Wrongly+blamed+https%3A%2F%2Fchromium-review.googlesource.com%2Fc%2Fchromium%2Fsrc%2F%2B%2F8520885&type=BUG

Original change's description:
> [Android] Re-enable AutoplayMutedNotificationTest on Desktop
>
> Previously, AutoplayMutedNotificationTest verified whether Chrome
> requested audio focus by registering an external
> OnAudioFocusChangeListener with AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN and
> checking if it was evicted (AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS).
>
> On Desktop Android builds where Multi Audio Focus is enabled
> (config_multi_audio_focus_enabled=true), multiple apps can hold
> AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN concurrently. As a result, the external listener was
> never evicted when Chrome requested audio focus in
> testUnmutedPlaybackTakesAudioFocus, causing it to time out, while
> muted playback tests vacuously passed their audio focus checks.
>
> Use HistogramWatcher on "Media.Android.AudioFocusRequestResult" to
> directly verify whether AudioFocusDelegate requested and was granted
> audio focus from AudioManager, and re-enable
> testUnmutedPlaybackTakesAudioFocus on Desktop Android. This avoids
> relying on internal OS resources or settings to query the multi-audio
> focus state, while ensuring both muted and unmuted focus behavior are
> verified regardless of the OS audio focus policy.
>
> Bug: 570100407, 570134281
> Change-Id: Ieb867168f4e2b3380cdf36619413609689fa4d6f
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8520885
> Reviewed-by: Frank Liberato <liberato@chromium.org>

parent
cf1c20511fd0972b9b177096180b85d8fc8f489b [ diff ] 
[webapps] Install any eligible page as DIY WebAPK

On Android (phones, tablets, and Android Desktop),
AddToHomescreenDataFetcher downgrades most pages without a promotable
manifest to a plain homescreen SHORTCUT, whereas Desktop Chrome
(Windows/Mac/Linux/ChromeOS) installs any eligible secure page as a DIY
app. This adds a minimal, flag-gated stopgap for Android and Android
Desktop designed to cherry-pick cleanly onto M155 and M156 without
altering InstallableManager's three-step GetData() sequencing:

- Behind kAndroidInstallAnyPageAsDiyAppStopgap (enabled by default on
  Android Desktop, disabled by default on phones/tablets):
  - Step 1 (metadata/manifest) omits broken manifest URLs from
    ShortcutInfo so WebAPK updates never fetch a 404/invalid manifest.
  - Step 2 (primary icon) sets is_debug_mode=true so cached manifest
    errors do not abort icon fetching, and continues to Step 3 when no
    icon is available (generating a monogram icon afterwards).
  - Step 3 (installability check) evaluates kImplicitManifestFieldsHTML
    and classifies errors: promotable manifests become WEBAPK/TWA,
    classification errors (missing/non-promotable manifest) become
    WEBAPK_DIY, and eligibility/reset/unknown errors remain SHORTCUT.
  - ShortcutInfo::UpdateDisplayMode(AppType) defaults WEBAPK_DIY to
    standalone on phones/tablets and minimal-ui on Android Desktop.
- Behind kInstallableRootFaviconFallback (enabled by default kill
  switch), InstallableIconFetcher probes <origin>/favicon.ico once on
  http(s) pages when all linked icon candidates fail, before falling
  back to an error or Android Desktop monogram.
- Adds two flag-independent crash guards (early return when WebContents
  is being destroyed, and posting the SHORTCUT fallback when Step 1
  receives a synchronous reset) and places observer OnDataAvailable
  calls last to prevent UAF on synchronous observer deletion.
- Adds Webapp.AddToHomescreen.AnyPage.{Decision,PrimaryIconSource}
  histograms, unit/instrumentation tests, and the design doc.

cd66a1809cbe80ca4b7f50a5dc4665fc9e8bdb15 [ diff ] 
Ttc: Move task creation and journal out of ToolController

In a followup CL we'll be passing the Journal to TTCMesClient (which now
lives under components/ttc and thus can't access the journal directly).
Without this change lifetimes become brittle since ToolController owning
meant it had to be destroyed after anything holding a reference to it.

(aesthetically, Journal doesn't belong in ToolController anyway and this
and moving task creation out also lets us use it in other ways if
needed)

8690a8978b0fd3a6aef641c50ec211fe084d2e33
parent
fa416834dd023f5b84f981f975a8a3b13fe5574b [ diff ] 
Cache Storage: one less yield in Size()

Cache storage scheduler operations can complete synchronously.

The old behavior can be traced to
https://codereview.chromium.org/1660233004/, which discusses a deadlock
issue, however that deadlock can't occur since CacheStorage no longer
runs on the IO thread (where the quota manager is). It seems likely that
the quota manager at the time required its size responses to be
delivered asynchronously, but there are plenty of other async steps here
these days.

tree
1e6ec8cb30e23618d59dcc2befae2343ca6391b4
parent
934e699839d13b1194a1ce3b393cff29d7949624 [ diff ] 
printing: Avoid probe stream in PrintSession#initiatePrintIfNeeded

A Printable representing a native PDF document is streamed directly to
the framework during onWrite(), bypassing renderer print mode. To check
whether the document is a PDF, initiatePrintIfNeeded() previously
called Printable#getPdfInputStream(), which opened a fresh InputStream
on every invocation and kept its underlying file descriptor open until
garbage collection.

Decouple PDF type classification from stream acquisition and latch the
document type once when the session starts. This avoids opening a probe
stream at startup and ensures onWrite() follows a consistent path even
if the tab navigates while the dialog is open or stream creation fails.

TAG=agy
CONV=481a86f4-576b-401a-b6ab-ac7b7a0fd3e1

Translate: Update always-translate visibility on language changes

Create the main and advanced always-translate checkboxes for the
TranslateBubble lifetime and update their visibility whenever the source
language changes. Keep the choose-language entry point and related spacing
in sync with the shortcut.

ResetLanguage restores the language selection programmatically, which does
not trigger SourceLanguageChanged(), so refresh the source-language-
dependent state explicitly there as well.

Add a regression test covering Unknown to known to Unknown transitions.

tree
f6e87549ec8fa57bcc127b0d143363ae0a834b43
parent
0ef5c0558b25bbbf6ea41c00e640f4af8bb0de58 [ diff ] 
ash: Update TabClusterUIClient via WebContentsObserver

As part of decoupling TabClusterUIClient from TabStripModelObserver
(b/369688254), this CL removes its dependency on OnTabChangedAt.
Instead, tab properties are observed directly:

- Title and URL: a WebContentsObserver per tab (TitleWasSet, and
  DidFinishNavigation for committed primary main frame navigations).
- Loading state: the new BrowserController::TabObserver::
  OnTabLoadingStateChanged, which BrowserControllerImpl forwards from
  TabChangeType::kLoadingOnly. That notification is sent synchronously
  for every loading state change, so the deferred kAll notification is
  not needed.

Behavior changes:

1. A tab item's source is now the tab's last committed URL rather than
   its visible URL. The visible URL may belong to a pending navigation
   (e.g. a URL typed into the omnibox that is still loading) or even to
   an aborted one (failed URLs stick around in the omnibox on the NTP),
   so it doesn't necessarily match the page that the tab shows.
   CoralDelegateImpl now also matches tabs by their last committed URL,
   so that a group's tabs are still found when moving them to a new
   desk.

2. Updates that don't change any of the item's fields are dropped.
   BirchCoralProvider already ignored them (see ShouldCreateEmbedding).

3. Title and URL updates are no longer delayed and coalesced by the
   browser's 200 ms UI update timer, which the kAll notification was
   subject to. As a result, BirchCoralProvider may request embeddings
   for intermediate states of a tab, e.g. when a page changes its URL
   and title in quick succession via the History API.

The browser window of a tab item is now determined once when the tab is
inserted, since it cannot change afterwards.

Also adds a browser test for TabClusterUIClient.

[omnibox] Add FullWebUIOmniboxLayoutHelper to keep CSS aligned

This CL introduces FullWebUIOmniboxLayoutHelper as the dedicated C++
layout helper for the Full WebUI Omnibox popup, serving as the single
source of truth for alignment insets, location bar height, icon sizes,
and font size.

- Reads layout constants from FullWebUIOmniboxFrame and GetLayoutConstant().
- Injects layout values dynamically into WebUIDataSource via loadTimeData.
- Removes isTouchUi_ and isMac_ from omnibox_popup_searchbox.ts and from
  loadTimeData, resolving crbug.com/519185419.
- Replaces hardcoded platform blocks in omnibox_popup_searchbox.css with
  dynamic CSS properties applied to host element style from loadTimeData.

Follow up work is still needed to make this dynamic, to support devices
that can switch between touch and non-touch modes, since `loadTimeData`
is only checked once per-element lifecycle.

Revert "Update KeyboardShortcuts to handle F7 consistently"

This reverts CL 7906098.

Restore page-first F7 handling on Android to address compatibility
regressions in enterprise web applications using physical keyboards.
Web content gets the first opportunity to handle F7, with caret
browsing handled through the original post-target onKeyDown() path.

This also removes browser-first F7 handling for secondary UI,
including ThinWebView.

Add a regression test verifying that dispatchKeyEvent() leaves F7
unhandled without requesting caret browsing. Restore the existing
test to verify the post-target caret browsing action, and clarify
its comments.

Also remove F7 from a later pre-handler comment, which no longer
describes the behavior after this revert.

7a532b0a863e4808de695075c311785082e17fd0 [ diff ] 
[Actor]: Register OpenKnownPageToolRequest in ToolRegistry

ToolRegistry provides a central catalog of browser tool schemas for
ActorKeyedService (go/actor-tool-registry-design). ToolRequest
subclasses expose their schema via static GetToolDefinition(), and
ToolRegistry discovers them at compile time through ToolRequestVariant.

Currently, OpenKnownPageToolRequest exists and is hooked up to the Actor
execution layer, but lacks a GetToolDefinition() implementation and
model-facing schema. As a result, ToolRegistry does not expose the
open_known_page tool.

This CL registers OpenKnownPageToolRequest in ToolRegistry by adding
ToolId::kOpenKnownPage, declaring kModelFacingName ("open_known_page")
and kQueryParam ("query"), and implementing GetToolDefinition() using
ToolDefinitionBuilder and ToolSchemaBuilder. It also adds unit test
coverage in tool_registry_unittest.cc under desktop build guards.

tree
0d279763c68e59aebd600a1009c1a7e2f77cfd94
parent
1bbc660a2d5e78731cefd6409bf9c268675afdce [ diff ] 
[a11y] Defer serialization until layout is clean

Computing accessible names with a dirty layout fails the CHECK in
MayHaveHTMLLabel(). Names are only computed during serialization,
when the cache is frozen and lifecycle transitions are not allowed,
so the layout cannot be updated at that point.

Check the state of the layout right before serialization. If the main
document (or popup) does not have a clean layout at that point,
SerializeAXUpdatesIfNeeded() calls ScheduleImmediateSerialization() and
returns before freezing and serializing the cache. The pending objects,
events, ready callbacks, etc. stay queued, waiting for the next
successful serialization.

Only serialization is deferred; commits are unchanged.

ScheduleImmediateSerialization() sets serialize_immediately_ and calls
ScheduleAXUpdate(), which requests a new frame. Because
serialize_immediately_ is set, the commit in that frame is forced, so
the retry does not wait for the batching delay.

ScheduleAXUpdate() may skip the frame request in some cases, for
example if the load is not yet complete; the pending updates then wait
for the next frame requested for any other reason.

Add a regression test.

3bd23a74d5c0ab22b0afe76f667eb1321d2100a9 [ diff ] 
[histogram-cleanup] Remove Media.VideoCapture.StreamShared

This removes the expired histogram Media.VideoCapture.StreamShared
(expired 2025-06-22), which recorded whether a camera was already being
shared when a new client connected in VideoCaptureManager. It expired
over a year ago and nobody needs the data anymore, so the recording call
and the metadata are dead weight. I dropped the UmaHistogramBoolean
call and deleted the histogram entry from the media histograms XML.

It also removes the expired histogram
Media.VideoCapture.StreamSharedSameOrigin (expired 2025-10-26), which
recorded whether the new client came from the same origin as the first
one, by dropping its UmaHistogramBoolean call and its XML entry. The
origin plumbing only existed to feed that metric, so I removed it too
(the origin parameter on ConnectClient and AddClient, the cached first
client origin, the origin lookup in MediaStreamManager and the matching
test arguments) and folded the now single-use client_exist check into
the if that uses it. Behavior is unchanged.

This change was generated using the skill histogram-cleanup.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Media.VideoCapture.StreamShared]=expired
OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Media.VideoCapture.StreamSharedSameOrigin]=expired

Bug: 499059525, 417216561, 445168949

Reland "[BottomSheet] Fix wrap-content measurement and viewport bottom inset"

This reverts commit 2500f440508f65f8bef5a9f1e6179f040e2e77e6.

Reason for revert: double counted E2E insets.

Original change's description:
> Revert "[BottomSheet] Fix wrap-content measurement and viewport bottom inset"
>
> This reverts commit 6430aa3a5ec3f69eba7dafe7f22d0569b92e4b93.
>
> Reason for revert: Suspected cause of breakage on android-12-x64-rel and android-10-x86-rel
>
> Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-10-x86-rel/b8668492391627102897
>
> Original change's description:
> > [BottomSheet] Fix wrap-content measurement and viewport bottom inset
> >
> > 1. In `getViewportBottomInset()`, guard against non-empty
> >         `mVisibleViewportRect` rather than `isSheetOpen()`. When
> >         opening a `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet in landscape (where
> >         `e2eBottomInset == 0` and navigation bar bottom inset is
> >         non-zero), `isSheetOpen()` was false during initial
> >         `showContent()` measurement, causing
> >         `getViewportBottomInset()` to return 0. Using
> >         `!mVisibleViewportRect.isEmpty()` ensures the bottom inset is
> >         accounted for immediately during initial wrap-content
> >         measurement. In `calculateSheetTranslationY()`, also zero
> >         `bottomInsetAdjustment` when `currentOffsetPx <= 0`.
> >
> > 2. In `updateViewportAndBottomInset()`, call
> >         `invalidateContentDesiredHeight()` when
> >         `insetOrHeightChanged` is true so container height or
> >         viewport inset changes seen by a layout pass trigger
> >         remeasurement of `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet content. In
> >         `onInsetChanged()`, do the same when the viewport bottom
> >         inset differs from the one seen by the last layout pass, and
> >         re-wrap an open `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet so that inset animations
> >         resettle it. `mPreviousViewportBottomInset` is still only
> >         updated by the layout pass, so the next layout pass still
> >         detects the change.
> >
> > 3. Add `getSystemBarOrEdgeToEdgeBottomInset()`, which returns
> >         the larger of the edge-to-edge bottom inset and the bottom
> >         system bars inset (or 0 for large form factor sheets and
> >         sheets with a bottom margin), without adding the keyboard
> >         height. Use it in `getContentBottomPadding()` and pass it to
> >         `mMediator.calculateSheetTranslationY()` in
> >         `setSheetOffsetFromBottom()`. In landscape mode where
> >         `e2eBottomInset` is 0 but navigation bar bottom inset is
> >         non-zero, this accounts for the bottom system bar inset. As
> >         with the edge-to-edge inset before, the translation also
> >         applies to `RESIZE_CONTENT` sheets.
> >
> > 4. In `ensureContentDesiredHeightIsComputed()`, subtract both
> >         `getHandlebarHeight()` and `getContentBottomPadding()` from
> >         `getMaxSheetHeight()` when measuring `WRAP_CONTENT` sheet
> >         content with `MeasureSpec.AT_MOST`.
> >
> > Before:
> > https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTExMzE2MDE5MjIyOTM3NnwwNTdhZDdjNi0wZg
> > https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjE1MDI3OTc2MTc1NjE2MHxkYzQxNTFiMC0wNA
> > After:
> > https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTM1MTY1MDA5NjA1NDI3Mnw0YWMxYTZiYS1hMg
> >
> > Bug: 530492213, 531670148

remoting: Extract InputInjectorMac header and replace Core with SequenceBound

Moves the InputInjectorMac class declaration to input_injector_mac.h, replaces the ref-counted InputInjectorMac::Core with base::SequenceBound<std::unique_ptr<Clipboard>> for clipboard_, and adds SEQUENCE_CHECKER and GUARDED_BY_CONTEXT annotations to InputInjectorMac.

audio: Add AudioManager::UsesQueuedOutputStream

Describe whether the output stream selected for the given parameters
enqueues buffers into an OS-managed playback queue instead of pulling
one buffer per hardware period. Such streams need data requests to use
the current callback's delay, including when starting or resuming,
instead of prefetching at zero delay.

IsMacAVFoundationPlaybackSupported() answers whether these parameters
select the Mac AVFoundation backend: macOS 27, the feature, PCM, and a
playback latency tag. AudioManagerMac reports the queued-stream
capability for that backend.

tree
85f61058306560c1d9625b294b94dd3cb7ea3893
parent
66fe4cc01e852bf482d1134f69c5ada284f97f13 [ diff ] 
[blinkpy] Do not rmtree($HOME) when LinuxPort.setup_test_run() fails

If `_start_xvfb()` fails during `LinuxPort.setup_test_run()`, it returns
`SYS_DEPS_EXIT_STATUS` before `_setup_dummy_home_dir()` is called,
leaving `self._original_home = None` and `HOME` pointing to the user's
real home directory.

When `wpt_adapter.py` exits its `test_env` context manager, it calls
`LinuxPort.clean_up_test_run()` -> `_clean_up_dummy_home_dir()`. Because
`dummy_home != None` evaluated to `True`, `_clean_up_dummy_home_dir()`
passed the user's real `$HOME` directory to `rmtree()`.

Fix this by storing `self._dummy_home_dir` explicitly when creating the
temporary directory and returning early in `_clean_up_dummy_home_dir()`
if no dummy home directory was created.

parent
c7a1f41d32fc3167defd4d46afe5fff29697c75a [ diff ] 
[CRD Linux] Add GdmRemoteUserDesktopSessionCreator

Adds RemoteUserDesktopSessionCreator, an interface for asking the system
to create a graphical session for a local user without interactive
authentication, and GdmRemoteUserDesktopSessionCreator, which implements
it via GDM's RemoteDisplayFactory.CreateUserDisplay (GDM 50+). Other
implementations, e.g. for a future XDG API, can be added later with GDM
as the fallback.

- All CreateUserDisplay errors fail the request. GDM not running or too
  old is detected via standard D-Bus error names, only for a clearer
  log message.
- The D-Bus call times out after 40 seconds, which is longer than
  callers are expected to wait for the session to show up.
- When GDM accepts the request, the log points at GDM's
  gdm-direct-login (or gdm-autologin) PAM service in case no session
  shows up. Currently GNOME 50 uses gdm-autologin for CreateUserDisplay,
  which causes problems since admins can't tell these sessions apart
  from unauthenticated autologin sessions. I'm introducing
  gdm-direct-login in
  https://gitlab.gnome.org/GNOME/gdm/-/merge_requests/406, but the exact
  PAM service name can change until the MR is merged.

UserDesktopSessionBackend will use it to implement
`createRemoteUserSessions` in a follow-up CL.

Unit tests cover detecting unsupported GDM.

afd366aad32a19bed46a2b7f517a63252fe459b3

Fri Oct 09 02:08:31 2026
tree
03dca2e8127738c2f8fd0fedf4acf6a13b45a468
parent
242d845a68d516aeb3da2c7faec549843afcbc5d [ diff ] 
[Collaboration] Clear UserData in MessagingBackendServiceImpl destructor

MessagingBackendService inherits from base::SupportsUserData and
attaches its Android JNI bridge (MessagingBackendServiceBridge) as
UserData. In ~MessagingBackendServiceBridge(), the bridge calls
service_->SetInstantMessageDelegate(nullptr) and
service_->RemovePersistentMessageObserver(this). Because
~MessagingBackendServiceImpl() was defaulted, attached UserData was
destroyed in ~SupportsUserData() after MessagingBackendServiceImpl's
members (instant_message_processor_ and persistent_message_observers_)
had already been destroyed and the vtable had unwound to the abstract
base class MessagingBackendService.

Explicitly call ClearAllUserData() in ~MessagingBackendServiceImpl() so
attached UserData instances are destroyed while
MessagingBackendServiceImpl is still alive, matching
DataSharingServiceImpl and TabGroupSyncServiceImpl.

TAG=agy
CONV=f1d2243d-815a-4bcc-b86b-3489af895752

fe4349e35fa7a2bc9719433784cf3055fca8f18e
parent
1a56e5ffff9d4fcb1880b436af6b857cbca300c7 [ diff ] 
network: Remove unused proxy_resolver.mojom import from url_response_head.mojom

Currently, //services/network/public/mojom:url_loader_base depends on
//services/proxy_resolver/public/mojom (which in turn depends on
:mojom_network_anonymization_key) solely because url_response_head.mojom
imports proxy_resolver.mojom. However, url_response_head.mojom does not
use any types from proxy_resolver.mojom.

Removing this unused import and public_dep allows url_loader_base (and
all downstream Blink mojom targets) to bypass 2 serial mojom parser
stages on the build critical path.

Also add the missing direct dependency on
:mojom_network_anonymization_key to //services/network/public/cpp:cpp_base,
which previously relied on the transitive dependency through
url_loader_base -> proxy_resolver.

494fd17e269d6c35e3bdeccd29b7f4a8ab05244c [ diff ] 
[Actor]: Register ExitFullscreenToolRequest in ToolRegistry

ToolRegistry provides a central catalog of browser tool schemas for
ActorKeyedService (go/actor-tool-registry-design). ToolRequest
subclasses expose their schema via static GetToolDefinition(), and
ToolRegistry discovers them at compile time through ToolRequestVariant.

Currently, ExitFullscreenToolRequest exists and is hooked up to the Actor
execution layer, but lacks a GetToolDefinition() implementation and
model-facing schema. As a result, ToolRegistry does not expose the
exit_fullscreen tool.

This CL registers ExitFullscreenToolRequest in ToolRegistry by adding
ToolId::kExitFullscreen, declaring kModelFacingName ("exit_fullscreen"),
and implementing GetToolDefinition() using ToolDefinitionBuilder. It also
adds unit test coverage in tool_registry_unittest.cc under desktop build guards.

tree
c3cc7e8dcff63daa2f0dda1c3ef3306b72f2e2c3
parent
efe060359aa892f6d110715e8f3a92ca485f513f [ diff ] 
omnibox: Don't create a contextual search session per popup

Every browser window builds its WebUI omnibox popups, and each
OmniboxPopupUI constructor created a contextual search session (a
ComposeboxQueryController with its own thread-pool task runner, plus a
metrics recorder) only to read the search content sharing pref. Most
popups never use the session, and the handlers already create it on
first use through GetOrCreateContextualSessionHandle().

Read the pref with ContextualSearchService::IsContextSharingEnabled()
instead. Creating the session later changes nothing: the metrics
recorder only logs for sessions that started, and handlers observe the
controller only once they ask for the session.

Also stop copying the composebox config protos while building the data
source.

TAG=agy
CONV=a03590ac-da78-4264-b0b6-3ac7536d2e06

parent
5781816cc56f49815c2f83da49babd23da5e1a41 [ diff ] 
Destroy LinkToTextIphController with its owner

TabbedRootUiCoordinator created LinkToTextIphController without keeping
a reference, and the controller had no teardown. Its CurrentTabObserver
was never destroyed, leaving its observer registered on both the
activity tab supplier and whichever Tab was active at teardown.

Make the controller Destroyable and have TabbedRootUiCoordinator own it
and destroy it in onDestroy(), matching the other IPH controllers it
manages. destroy() also guards the asynchronous selector lookup so a
late result cannot show a message for a destroyed controller.

TAG=agy
CONV=8569c58a-12db-4404-a291-3c9e3486edf5

[Surface Embed] Pinch zoom (Part 3/3): Route input

Parts 1 and 2 introduced pinch ownership resolution and configured it
through allow-pinch-zoom.

This change routes touchscreen pinch sequences to the resolved owner and
keep that owner latched through the gesture. Preserve OOPIF filtering,
synthesize wrapping scroll events when required, transform coordinates
into the target, and clean up state when views or sequences end.
Existing embeds continue using root scaling unless they opt in.

[Vertical Tabs] Fix rail expand-on-hover edge cases

- Collapse the rail when the pointer leaves the window straight from it.
  Android sends the same HOVER_EXIT inside the rail for this and for a
  mouse press, so collapse after 100 ms unless a press follows.
- Don't hover-expand the rail while it animates to collapsed, which made
  it flicker after clicking the collapse button.
- Hover-expand a collapsed rail only after the pointer stays over it for
  100 ms, so moving quickly across it doesn't expand then collapse it.

Known issue: the system takes over the window's left edge for resizing,
even when maximized, so the rail collapses there. Desktop Chrome doesn't
allow edge resizing when maximized.

Demo: http://screencast/cast/NTE1NTQ0MzU0MTI3ODcyMHw1Zjg4YmQwOC00Ng

Bug: 571238818, 571237651

6943f13ae3bf8632d6b71d628da2856660a06718

tree
28f25a33f1b4cc7367d30ee00f59332687293304
parent
23862a482469e7bd2143e06f99756794c4e240f6 [ diff ] 
Use LazyRE2 instead of RE2 for static regexes

LazyRE2 can be declared as a constexpr compile-time.

9949cdf846d33f9e6db2e45602e0ab21c49380e2

Fri Oct 09 01:24:11 2026
tree
33fec02ca31b41440e708e8673c92bc2fbd67e94
parent
61b98cdcbba4cec584db660c7548a1067795188b [ diff ] 
contextual_cueing: Observe the tab list only while a cue depends on other tabs

Every tab has a ContextualCueingController, and each one observed the
window's TabListInterface from its constructor for as long as the tab was
in a window. The observation exists only to hide a shown cue when a tab
it depends on is closed (OnTabRemoved), but it made every tab insertion,
removal and move notify one observer per tab: inserting N tabs into a
window is O(N^2). Restoring a session with 1555 tabs spent 1.1 s in
TabListBridge's insert fan-out, 0.06 ms for the first tabs and 1.4 ms
per tab once ~1500 controllers were observing.

Observe the tab list only from ShowCue(), once the cue is actually shown
and depends on other tabs, and stop when the cue is hidden. Detaching
the tab already resets the observation and hides a cue that has
dependencies, so a tab never arrives in a window with dependencies left
over; OnTabInserted() now checks that instead of observing again.

Measured on a component build (Windows, ContextualCueingV2 enabled by the
field trial testing config), session restore of 1555 tabs:
TabListBridge insert fan-out 1.1 s -> 0.02 s (two runs), i.e. the
tab-strip observer notification for each inserted tab 8.9 ms -> 8.2 ms
on average and 14.9 ms -> 13.6 ms once ~1500 tabs were present.

[Reading mode] Pass speech rate to menus as a prop

rate-change, reads the value back into a speechRate prop and passes it
through the toolbar to the rate menu. The toolbar's rate button reads
the same prop, so it no longer listens for the rate event or the
restore-from-prefs callback. The menu only renders the prop and fires
events, so speechRate is dropped from SettingsPrefs.

ed2a96b55a1ef4a3ebfba37ccaebd9f363ceff19
parent
394894e7292d1aa5bb90933e63edb768320abcdc [ diff ] 
[ChroMenu] Fix ActionAppMenuTest.PopulatesBookmarksSubmenu on official builds

https://crrev.com/c/8495039 updated
BookmarksDynamicMenu::AddBookmarkFolderAction to set
AppMenuActionItem::kIsSubmenuKey instead of manually adding a disabled
child ActionItem with IDS_MENU_EMPTY_SUBMENU, so empty bookmark folders
populate a views::EmptyMenuMenuItem (which uses
IDS_APP_MENU_EMPTY_SUBMENU).

On builds with enable_resource_allowlist_generation = true (such as
official Windows builds), IDS_MENU_EMPTY_SUBMENU is no longer referenced
in chrome.dll and is stripped from resources.pak, causing
l10n_util::GetStringUTF16(IDS_MENU_EMPTY_SUBMENU) in
ActionAppMenuTest.PopulatesBookmarksSubmenu to DCHECK-fail.

Update ActionAppMenuTest.PopulatesBookmarksSubmenu to expect
IDS_APP_MENU_EMPTY_SUBMENU, matching views::EmptyMenuMenuItem.

Example failure:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/win64/138748/test-results?q=ID%3A%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Aunit_tests%21gtest%3A%3AActionAppMenuTest%23PopulatesBookmarksSubmenu+V%3Abuilder%3Dwin64+V%3Atest_suite%3Dunit_tests

Move PrefsTabHelper from AttachTabHelpers to TabFeatures

Move PrefsTabHelper::CreateForWebContents from
TabHelpers::AttachTabHelpers to Desktop and Android TabFeatures, and
recreate it on new_contents in TabFeatures::WillDiscardContents.

ChromeExtensionHostDelegate, Glic WebContents,
WebUIContentsPreloadManager, and WebUIContentsWrapper).

2251fb01ebc5acd194279abfde5af11ae511f238 [ diff ] 
[focus-without-user-activation] Fix label forwarding

Preserve the calling frame when a label forwards focus to its associated
control, both for label.focus() and scripted click activation. Losing
the caller caused these paths to check the target document's permissions
policy instead.

Forward FocusParams::initiator_frame from HTMLLabelElement::Focus(). For
untrusted click events, use the incumbent window's frame as the
initiator, matching Element::focusForBindings(). Trusted clicks (user
input, accessibility, UA-simulated) still use the label's frame.

The new WPT is tentative while initiator attribution is specified:
https://github.com/whatwg/html/issues/12032

Revert "[DCSI] Use TLS SharedContextState in WrappedSkImageBacking"

This reverts commit f946afc0e6222cecd5e7a642e6eb43d98653c01b.

Reason for revert: Seems to be crashing the emulator in
android-15-tablet-x64-rel

Failure Link:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-15-tablet-x64-rel/9940/overview

Original change's description:
> [DCSI] Use TLS SharedContextState in WrappedSkImageBacking
>
> UploadFromMemory() and ReadbackToMemory() previously used the creation
> thread's SharedContextState directly, which is not safe when a
> thread-safe backing is accessed on another thread. CompoundImageBacking
> needs to copy between backings on any thread, e.g. the DrDC
> CompositorGpuThread, using CPU readback/upload as a fallback.
>
> Use the creation thread's SharedContextState when on the creation
> thread or when the backing is not thread-safe (such as on Android
> WebView where DrDC is disabled and no thread-local SharedContextState
> is set), and use the SharedContextState registered for the current
> thread when accessed on another thread. Also call
> GrDirectContext::submit() after UploadFromMemory() when the backing is
> thread-safe so that Vulkan upload commands are submitted before another
> thread reads the texture.
>
> Bug: 535630340
> Change-Id: Ib68ab639d81b0e3bf6ac3d140dc0467e05a1e9a9
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8518438
> Reviewed-by: Vasiliy Telezhnikov <vasilyt@chromium.org>
> Commit-Queue: vikas soni <vikassoni@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1714887}

679ae8ef771c5c410a46de0af1c457120954334f
parent
a328d16efe4b473704ee8272450de149ada52951 [ diff ] 
settings: Open chrome://settings/toolbar without its arguments

Problem:
"Edit shortcut" on the toolbar shortcut button opens the toolbar page,
however that page's bundle arguments are not registered as url arguments
causing the page to fallback to the default /account or /googleServices
page.

There are several ways to fix this (e.g., register the url arguments)
but after doing some research, they don't seem to be needed, and
registering them can cause more problems than it solves. Without the
args present, the Toolbar page computes them on its own using
AdaptiveToolbarStatePredictor. Therefore, the four args we drop are:

- ranked_toolbar_button_states: never read by the page.
  RadioButtonGroupAdaptiveToolbarPreference#initButtonsFromUiState()
  ignores it.

- can_show_ui: only checked for presence, as a marker that the
  arguments came from the toolbar. Its value is never read.

- preference_selection: the selected radio button. It comes only from
  the saved preference and whether that button is available, so the
  page's own predictor computes the same value.

- auto_button_caption: the "Current recommendation" line under "Based
  on your usage". The page computes it with the default
  AdaptiveToolbarBehavior instead of TabbedAdaptiveToolbarBehavior, so
  it can differ only when the usage model's top choice is a button the
  toolbar hides.

Because the arguments are a snapshot, they also must not be replayed
when the Url is restored from history or on tab restore, where
preference_selection would override a preference changed since.

Add RouteSpec#dropArgsFromUrl() for such routes, and use it for
/toolbar. createUrlForFragment() leaves the arguments out of the Url,
urlPreservesArgs() accepts that Url, and resolve() ignores any query
typed onto it.

Materially, under URL navigation "Edit shortcut" now opens
chrome://settings/toolbar directly. When settings is not shown in a tab
or URL navigation is off, the Intent still carries the arguments as
before.

[ContextualTasks] Clear uncommitted tab underlines on input clear

When a user adds tabs to the composebox input plate and clicks
clear to discard uncommitted inputs, refresh the active task
context for unsubmitted tabs while preserving the composebox
favicon stack and tab-strip underlines for submitted tabs:

- In ContextualSearchboxHandler::ClearFiles(), retain submitted
  tokens in selected_tabs and only remove underlines and entries
  for unsubmitted tokens when query_submitted is false, then
  notify active_task_context_provider->RefreshContext().
- In ContextualTasksComposeboxHandler::ClearFiles(), clear
  delayed_tabs_ and block auto-suggested tabs before calling
  ComposeboxHandler::ClearFiles() so RefreshContext() sees the
  updated sources. Also reset the session handle before clearing
  files in OnTaskChanged().
- In ContextualSearchSessionHandle, add
  GetActiveSubmittedContextTokens() combining
  submitted_context_tokens_ with submitted entries in
  tab_context_.attached (excluding deselected tabs), and use it
  in SubmittedContextDecorator and
  GetSubmittedContextFileInfos().
- In AiModeContextLibraryConverter, fall back to matching
  uploaded FileInfo by tab_url when context_id changes after AIM
  postMessage so UrlResource::tab_id is preserved.
- In ComposeboxEmbedderMixin::clearAllInputs(), introduce
  shouldResetRestoredTabs() so restored tabs are only cleared
  when submitting a query with context management disabled. Also
  pass ShouldClearAllInputsOnSubmit() in ContextualTasksUI load
  time data.

811d63d9fa4eed52cb922b6cf1d22a855e6c741f [ diff ] 
[Android] Re-enable AutoplayMutedNotificationTest on Desktop

Previously, AutoplayMutedNotificationTest verified whether Chrome
requested audio focus by registering an external
OnAudioFocusChangeListener with AudioManager.AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN and
checking if it was evicted (AUDIOFOCUS_LOSS).

On Desktop Android builds where Multi Audio Focus is enabled
(config_multi_audio_focus_enabled=true), multiple apps can hold
AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN concurrently. As a result, the external listener was
never evicted when Chrome requested audio focus in
testUnmutedPlaybackTakesAudioFocus, causing it to time out, while
muted playback tests vacuously passed their audio focus checks.

Use HistogramWatcher on "Media.Android.AudioFocusRequestResult" to
directly verify whether AudioFocusDelegate requested and was granted
audio focus from AudioManager, and re-enable
testUnmutedPlaybackTakesAudioFocus on Desktop Android. This avoids
relying on internal OS resources or settings to query the multi-audio
focus state, while ensuring both muted and unmuted focus behavior are
verified regardless of the OS audio focus policy.

9251c09a06397eb83cfb1ba3b07a15f62f4eaa29
parent
d45f6970a6950b6cdee9e4f28213c24b31d44a8c [ diff ] 
Support 'auto' tracking mode for Android window occlusion

Introduce 'auto' mode for the AndroidWindowOcclusion tracking_mode
feature parameter.

When 'auto' is selected, Chrome uses the new strict
TrustedPresentationListener API if supported on the platform;
otherwise, it automatically falls back to self_occlusion mode. This
allows enabling the Finch experiment broadly without disabling occlusion
tracking on older Android versions where the strict API is unavailable.

aa70c8bd8e2d0a8ef604055bedff1e929ab189e9 [ diff ] 
[Notifications] SW popup capability delegation

When handling a notification click, a Service Worker may need an
existing client window to open a popup (e.g. an email preview or chat
compose). Currently, window.open() in the client is blocked by the popup
blocker because the client window lacks transient user activation.

This CL allows the Service Worker to delegate popup capability to a
same-origin WindowClient via client.postMessage(msg, {delegate:
'popup'}). Upon receipt, the client window receives a 1-second transient
popup token enabling window.open(). This is decoupled from window
interaction and gated behind the CapabilityDelegationPopup feature flag.

See https://chromestatus.com/feature/5091965583622144

Bug: 542314185, b:529458813
Test: http/tests/serviceworker/clients-capability-delegation.html
Test: external/wpt/html/capability-delegation/
Test: blink_unittests --gtest_filter=WebFrameTest.CapabilityDelegation*

[Surface Embed] Pinch zoom (Part 1/3): Resolve pinch zoom target

Touchscreen pinch gestures currently scale the root view, including when
they originate in embedded or out-of-process content. Before input
routing can selectively scale embedded content, each view needs a
consistent way to identify which WebContents should own the gesture.

Add target resolution that preserves the root view as the default.
OOPIF child views delegate through their frame connector, while Surface
Embed connectors can either select their child or continue toward the
embedder. This also supports resolving ownership through nested embeds.

Later changes will configure this policy and consume the resolved target
during input dispatch.

Bug: 568317234
Test: SurfaceEmbedConnectorImplBrowserTest.*
Test: RenderWidgetHostViewChildFrameTest.*

a2dd931e8feca1f5dec4938d1fe5751d142e67fe

tree
bd441ef923f7d2d2911cacffed012a23c868933c
parent
e9cc36f74bd414a0d20c25123588e8c0a4e6e171 [ diff ] 
Gate D3D11VideoDecoderUseSharedHandle on single texture decoders.

Shared handle output textures cannot be presented with the zero-copy
decode swap chain, so enabling D3D11VideoDecoderUseSharedHandle forces
decoders that would otherwise decode into a texture array, e.g. H.264,
onto the VPBlit presentation path and regresses playback power.

Only apply shared handles when the driver requests one texture per
picture buffer (bit 14 of ConfigDecoderSpecific), where the decode
swap chain is unavailable anyway.

Also add the D3D11VideoDecoderUseSharedHandle to fieldtrial config so
win10 perf tests will cover it.

5c43e7f8462d291512703caf1860877c12f80774 [ diff ] 
Deflake cdata-001.xhtml and cdata-002.xhtml by waiting for Ahem

`wpt/html/rendering/cdata-001.xhtml` and
`wpt/html/rendering/cdata-002.xhtml` are flaky due to not waiting for
Ahem to load. When the tests are run by themselves, the flakiness is
consistently reproducible without this patch and goes away with this
patch, which waits for the font to be ready. This patch has a small
special-case because the fonts may not load until the non-empty xhtml
with the Ahem font is present, so a second await is needed.

[OE] Align plus button icon with suggestion icons and submit button

The 40px-wide oblong contextual entrypoint (+) button in #bottomControls
previously used a 16px inline-start padding (plus a 0.5px margin in
composebox), placing the centered 20px plus icon at 36px/36.5px instead
of 32px where the 32px-wide suggestion icon containers (16px + 16px) are
centered.

Update #bottomControls padding-inline-start to 12px and remove the 0.5px
inline-start margin on #contextEntrypoint so the plus icon center aligns
at 32px with the suggestion and input icons. Also set padding-inline-end
to 12px on #bottomControls when cr-composebox-submit is present so the
40px-wide submit button maintains matching 12px horizontal edge spacing.

Demo: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTcwMjQxNTc4MDE1MTI5NnwzZjA4ZTA0NC04Yw

build: Make chrome/linux cross builds on mac hosts work

On mac hosts, pkg-config.py now queries the linux sysroot's .pc files
with the host's pkg-config instead of returning empty results, so the
right include dirs, libs and defines (e.g. the ATSPI version) are used.
Also disable the V8 context snapshot for linux builds on non-linux
hosts, as is already done for mac and win cross builds.

Tested by building chrome for linux arm64 and linux x64 on a mac arm64
host (with target_os = ["linux"] in .gclient).

3e28c1a2a21e84289ce498bce9418e0452130ef9 [ diff ] 
Android: Skip extracting .res.zip files in compile_resources.py

Move static resource path transformations (locale directory renaming,
moving images from drawable-*-mdpi-* folders to non-mdpi folders, and
stripping .png/.webp extensions) from compile_resources.py into
prepare_resources.py, and stop adding {index}_{resource_dir_name}/
prefixes and MULTIPLE_RES_MAGIC_STRING zip comments when creating
.resources.zip archives.

In compile_resources.py, pass unmodified .resources.zip files directly
to 'aapt2 compile --zip', and create a temporary filtered input zip only
for dependencies that have entries excluded by
--resource-exclusion-regex or --locale-allowlist, or converted by
--png-to-webp.

Benchmark (hyperfine --warmup 1 --runs 5 on
chrome_public_apk__compile_resources):
  Before: 19.673 s +- 0.208 s (user: 15.474 s, sys: 12.310 s)
  After:  11.365 s +- 0.097 s (user: 13.650 s, sys:  7.277 s)
  Speedup: 1.73x faster (-8.31 s / -42.2%)

tree
2a363a7a4354798bcaa9e7aa55fee900869d9226
parent
50d79f65913c295a6c6c8ad8cad1aaa37985b3f9 [ diff ] 
mojo: Cache PLY lexer and yacc parser across Parse() calls in parser.py

Previously, Parse() called lex.lex() and yacc.yacc() on every
invocation, rebuilding the regex lexer tables and LALR(1) parsing tables
from scratch for every .mojom file parsed in the same process.

Build the Lexer/lex and Parser/yacc instances once per process and
reset their per-file state (filename, comment lists, lineno, and lexer
state) on each Parse() call.

Measured median wall time improvements:
- //third_party/blink/public/mojom:mojom_platform__parser_action (249
  files):
  - Default parallelism (multi-core): 1.45s -> 1.41s (-0.04s, -2.6%)
  - Single-core (taskset -c 0): 6.29s -> 4.89s (-1.40s, -22.3%)
- //content/common:mojo_bindings__parser_action (18 files, single-core):
  2.27s -> 2.18s (-0.08s, -3.6%)
- Mojom python unit tests (221 tests, single-threaded):
  5.81s -> 0.49s (-5.32s, -91.6%, 11.8x faster)
